- **subsystems**: A **Subsystem** is a `UObject`-derived singleton whose lifetime matches a specific engine construct (engine process, game instance, world, or local player). The engine creates it automatically when the owning object is created and destroys it when the owner is torn down — no manual instancing, no GC juggling, and Blueprint/Python exposure comes for free. ([ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game](https://github.com/ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/subsystems) · ★1)
- **subsystems**: Load when installing or wiring an infrastructure subsystem in a project that uses @pattern-stack/codegen — events, jobs, cache, storage, integration, bridge, observability, auth, or the OpenAPI config. Covers `codegen subsystem install`, the `forRoot` registration ORDER in app.module.ts, which subsystems depend on which, and multi-tenancy opt-in. Get the order wrong and the bridge sits idle or observability sees nothing — this skill is the source of truth for ordering until the CLI enforces it. ([pattern-stack/codegen-patterns](https://github.com/pattern-stack/codegen-patterns/tree/HEAD/consumer-skills/subsystems))

- **subtask-pipeline**: The "planner -&gt; executor -&gt; review" pipeline: the strong model (Opus) writes the plan and the subtask brief, the weaker and cheaper one (Sonnet) carries it out in a subagent, and the strong one checks the result and accepts it or sends it back for rework. Use for any task from the plan that can be handed over whole: laying out a frame, an IPC command, a core test, a mock edit, screenshots, docs. Triggers: подзадача, subtask, подагент, subagent, Sonnet, исполнитель (executor), делегировать (delegate), бриф (brief), "отдай слабой модели" (hand it to the weaker model). - **subtext**: Read what a message is actually saying, using the structure of the text as evidence - what is missing, what is hedged, what got skipped, where the temperature changes.
